# 自动建造前36个建筑（temple ~ fort6）
country_event = {
	id = 202600
	trigger = {
		tag = NPC
		any_country = {
			any_owned_province = {
				not = { has_province_modifier = zhengzaijianshe }   # 不在建设中
				has_province_flag = zhengzaijianshe                  # 有待建标记
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = { months = 0 }
	title = "EVTNAME202600"
	desc = "EVTDESC202600"

	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA202600"

		# temple
		any_country = {
			limit = {
				any_owned_province = {
					not = { has_province_modifier = zhengzaijianshe }
					has_province_flag = zidongzhongtian_temple
				}
			}
			any_owned = {
				limit = {
					not = { has_province_modifier = zhengzaijianshe }
					has_province_flag = zidongzhongtian_temple
				}
				clr_province_flag = zidongzhongtian_temple
				clr_province_flag = zhengzaijianshe
				add_building = temple
			}
		}

		# courthouse
		any_country = {

country_event = {
	id = 202601
	trigger = {
		tag = NPC
		any_country = {
			or = {
				has_country_flag = zidongzhongtian_jingji
				has_country_flag = zidongzhongtian_junshi
				has_country_flag = zidongzhongtian_wenhua
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = { months = 0 }
	title = "EVTNAME202600"
	desc = "EVTDESC202600"

	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA202600"

		# ==================== 农业（科技：government_tech） ====================

				limit = {
					has_province_flag = zidongzhongtian_yaozhong_shangye
					not = { has_building = marketplace }
					owner = { OR = { AND = { has_country_flag = zidongzhongtian_yusuan_5000 treasury = 5100 officials = 11 } AND = { has_country_flag = zidongzhongtian_yusuan_2000 treasury = 2100 officials = 11 } AND = { has_country_flag = zidongzhongtian_yusuan_700 treasury = 800 officials = 11 } } }
				}
				set_province_flag = zidongzhongtian_marketplace
				set_province_flag = zhengzaijianshe
				add_province_modifier = { name = "zhengzaijianshe" duration = 90 }
				owner = { treasury = -100 officials = -1 }
				clr_province_flag = zidongzhongtian_yaozhong_shangye

					has_province_flag = zidongzhongtian_yaozhong_wenhua
					not = { has_building = naval_base }
					owner = { OR = { AND = { has_country_flag = zidongzhongtian_yusuan_5000 treasury = 5500 officials = 13 } AND = { has_country_flag = zidongzhongtian_yusuan_2000 treasury = 2500 officials = 13 } AND = { has_country_flag = zidongzhongtian_yusuan_700 treasury = 1200 officials = 13 } } }
				}
				set_province_flag = zidongzhongtian_naval_base
				set_province_flag = zhengzaijianshe
				add_province_modifier = { name = "zhengzaijianshe" duration = 1080 }

		}

		# ==================== 清理 ====================
		any_country = {
			any_owned = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_province_flag = zidongzhongtian_yaozhong_nongye
						has_province_flag = zidongzhongtian_yaozhong_gongye
						has_province_flag = zidongzhongtian_yaozhong_shangye
						has_province_flag = zidongzhongtian_yaozhong_junshi
						has_province_flag = zidongzhongtian_yaozhong_wenhua
					}
				}
				clr_province_flag = zidongzhongtian_yaozhong_nongye
				clr_province_flag = zidongzhongtian_yaozhong_gongye
				clr_province_flag = zidongzhongtian_yaozhong_shangye
				clr_province_flag = zidongzhongtian_yaozhong_junshi
				clr_province_flag = zidongzhongtian_yaozhong_wenhua
			}
		}

country_event = {
	id = 202605
	trigger = {
		# 任意国家 任意省份 满足2种以上五级建筑就触发
		any_country = {
			any_owned_province = {
				OR = {
					AND = { has_building = college has_building = arsenal }
					AND = { has_building = college has_building = naval_arsenal }
					AND = { has_building = college has_building = mint }
					AND = { has_building = college has_building = post_office }
					AND = { has_building = college has_building = fort_5 }
					
					AND = { has_building = arsenal has_building = naval_arsenal }
					AND = { has_building = arsenal has_building = mint }
					AND = { has_building = arsenal has_building = post_office }
					AND = { has_building = arsenal has_building = fort_5 }
					
					AND = { has_building = naval_arsenal has_building = mint }
					AND = { has_building = naval_arsenal has_building = post_office }
					AND = { has_building = naval_arsenal has_building = fort_5 }
					
					AND = { has_building = mint has_building = post_office }
					AND = { has_building = mint has_building = fort_5 }
					
					AND = { has_building = post_office has_building = fort_5 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = { months = 0 }
	title = "EVTNAME202605"
	desc = "EVTDESC202605"

	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA202605"

		# 严格按你要求的结构
		any_country = {
			limit = {
				# 筛选国家：这个国家 有省份满足2种五级建筑
				any_owned_province = {
					OR = {
						AND = { has_building = college has_building = arsenal }
						AND = { has_building = college has_building = naval_arsenal }
						AND = { has_building = college has_building = mint }
						AND = { has_building = college has_building = post_office }
						AND = { has_building = college has_building = fort_5 }
						
						AND = { has_building = arsenal has_building = naval_arsenal }
						AND = { has_building = arsenal has_building = mint }
						AND = { has_building = arsenal has_building = post_office }
						AND = { has_building = arsenal has_building = fort_5 }
						
						AND = { has_building = naval_arsenal has_building = mint }
						AND = { has_building = naval_arsenal has_building = post_office }
						AND = { has_building = naval_arsenal has_building = fort_5 }
						
						AND = { has_building = mint has_building = post_office }
						AND = { has_building = mint has_building = fort_5 }
						
						AND = { has_building = post_office has_building = fort_5 }
					}
				}
			}
			# 筛选满足条件的省份并删除
			any_owned = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = { has_building = college has_building = arsenal }
						AND = { has_building = college has_building = naval_arsenal }
						AND = { has_building = college has_building = mint }
						AND = { has_building = college has_building = post_office }
						AND = { has_building = college has_building = fort_5 }
						
						AND = { has_building = arsenal has_building = naval_arsenal }
						AND = { has_building = arsenal has_building = mint }
						AND = { has_building = arsenal has_building = post_office }
						AND = { has_building = arsenal has_building = fort_5 }
						
						AND = { has_building = naval_arsenal has_building = mint }
						AND = { has_building = naval_arsenal has_building = post_office }
						AND = { has_building = naval_arsenal has_building = fort_5 }
						
						AND = { has_building = mint has_building = post_office }
						AND = { has_building = mint has_building = fort_5 }
						
						AND = { has_building = post_office has_building = fort_5 }
					}
				}
				# 删除该省全部五级建筑（含要塞）
				remove_building = college
				remove_building = arsenal
				remove_building = naval_arsenal
				remove_building = mint
				remove_building = post_office
				remove_building = fort_5
			}
		}
	}
